Implement the following plan:

# Fix: Codecov Base Upload workflow 404

## Context
The "Codecov Base Upload" workflow (`codecov-base.yml`) fails every time it runs on push to `main`. The step "Download coverage from latest CI run" gets a 404 from the GitHub API. This means Codecov never gets base branch coverage, so PR coverage diffs don't work.

## Root Cause
In `.github/workflows/codecov-base.yml` line 27-29, the `gh api` call uses `-f per_page=5`. The `-f` flag causes `gh api` to switch from GET to POST. The GitHub API endpoint (`/actions/workflows/{id}/runs`) only accepts GET, returning 404 for POST.

## Fix
**File:** `.github/workflows/codecov-base.yml`

Change line 27-29 from:
```bash
RUN_ID=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/workflows/ci.yml/runs \
  --jq '[.workflow_runs[] | select(.conclusion == "success")][0].id' \
  -f per_page=5)
```
To:
```bash
RUN_ID=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/workflows/ci.yml/runs?per_page=5" \
  --jq '[.workflow_runs[] | select(.conclusion == "success")][0].id')
```

No other `gh api` calls in the file use `-f`, so this is the only line that needs changing.

## Verification
- Push the fix to a branch, merge to `main`, and confirm the Codecov Base Upload workflow passes
- Or: manually run the corrected `gh api` command to confirm it returns a valid run ID (already verified locally)


If you need specific details from before exiting plan mode (like exact code snippets, error messages, or content you generated), read the full transcript at: /Users/michael/.claude/projects/-Users-michael-Code-cipher-box/b49a3c21-2120-460f-9623-2f335bfeb258.jsonl

   690→ * Unpin a single file's content and metadata CIDs, and update quota.
   691→ *
   692→ * Prefers stored contentCid (captured at soft-delete time) to avoid
   693→ * re-decrypting encrypted file metadata (which was the GAP-1 bug).
   694→ * Falls back to legacy IPNS resolution for old entries without contentCid.
   695→ */
   696→async function unpinFileCids(entry: BinEntry): Promise<void> {
   697→  try {
   698→    // Preferred path: use stored contentCid from soft-delete time
   699→    if (entry.contentCid) {
   700→      await unpinFromIpfs(entry.contentCid);
   701→      if (entry.contentSize && typeof entry.contentSize === 'number') {
   702→        useQuotaStore.getState().removeUsage(entry.contentSize);
   703→      }
   704→      // Also unpin the file metadata CID
   705→      if (entry.filePointer) {
   706→        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(entry.filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName);
   707→        if (resolved?.cid) {
   708→          await unpinFromIpfs(resolved.cid).catch(() => {});
   709→        }
   710→      }
   711→      return;
   712→    }
   713→
   714→    // Fallback for legacy entries without contentCid:
   715→    // Resolve IPNS and attempt to parse file metadata as plaintext JSON.
   716→    // NOTE: This will fail for encrypted file metadata (the original GAP-1 bug).
   717→    // Legacy entries that hit this path will log an error but not crash.
   718→    if (!entry.filePointer) return;
   719→    const fileIpnsName = entry.filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName;

I see three places that need version unpinning:

1. `unpinFileCids` preferred path (line 699-711) — needs to also resolve file metadata to get versions
2. `unpinFileCids` fallback path (line 727-734) — already has parsed `fileMeta`, just needs version loop
3. `cleanupFolderCids` (line 782-788) — already has decrypted `fileMeta`, just needs version loop

Since the version unpin logic is the same everywhere, let me create a small helper to avoid duplication. Actually, it's simple enough to inline. Let me implement it.

For the preferred path in `unpinFileCids`, the function already resolves IPNS at line 706 but only to unpin the metadata CID. However, it doesn't decrypt the file metadata (it can't — it doesn't have the folder key). So for the preferred path, we can't access versions without the folder key. > AGENT

We have `userPrivateKey` available. The issue is that `unpinFileCids` for top-level files doesn't have the folder key to decrypt file metadata and get versions. But it does resolve the file's IPNS already.

The cleanest approach: since we already capture `contentCid`/`contentSize` at soft-delete time, we should also capture version CIDs then. But that's a schema change. The simpler approach is to thread a folder key into `unpinFileCids` when available, and for the preferred path, resolve + decrypt the file metadata to get versions.

Actually — for top-level file bin entries, we have `userPrivateKey` and the entry's `originalParentIpnsName`. We could look up the folder key from the store. But that's fragile. The better approach: `unpinFileCids` already resolves the file's IPNS in the preferred path. If we also fetch and decrypt the file metadata there, we need the folder key. The folder key is ECIES-wrapped with the user's public key and stored in the parent folder... which we may not have.

Let me check what's simplest — add a helper that unpins versions from a decrypted `fileMeta`, and use it in the two places where we already have decrypted metadata (`cleanupFolderCids` and `unpinFileCids` fallback). For the preferred path, we'd need to also decrypt.

Perfect. I now have all the information needed. Let me provide a comprehensive report on the VersionEntry type and how file versioning relates to the bin/permanent-delete flow.

## Summary: VersionEntry Type and Versioning in Bin/Permanent-Delete Flow

### 1. VersionEntry Type Definition

**Location:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/crypto/src/file/types.ts:10-23`

```typescript
export type VersionEntry = {
  /** IPFS CID of the encrypted file content for this version */
  cid: string;
  /** Hex-encoded ECIES-wrapped AES-256 key for decrypting this version */
  fileKeyEncrypted: string;
  /** Hex-encoded IV used for this version's encryption */
  fileIv: string;
  /** Original file size in bytes (before encryption) */
  size: number;
  /** When this version was created (Unix ms) -- i.e., when it became a "past" version */
  timestamp: number;
  /** Encryption mode used for this version */
  encryptionMode: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
};
```

**Key characteristics:**
- Each version stores **complete crypto material** for independent decryption (CID, wrapped key, IV)
- `size` and `timestamp` track the version's metadata
- `encryptionMode` is **required** (not optional), unlike FileMetadata where it defaults to 'GCM'
- Versions are stored as an **optional array** in FileMetadata, newest first (or omitted if no versions exist)

---

### 2. FileMetadata Versions Field

**Location:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/crypto/src/file/types.ts:49-50`

```typescript
/** Past versions of this file (newest first). Omitted if no versions exist. */
versions?: VersionEntry[];
```

**In the document:**
- Referenced at line 205 of `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/docs/METADATA_SCHEMAS.md`
- Schema allows up to **10 versions maximum per file**
- 15-minute cooldown between version creation
- This is an **optional field with default omitted** — no version bump required

---

### 3. Rust Equivalent (Desktop App)

**Location:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/folder.rs:148-166`

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct VersionEntry {
    pub cid: String,
    pub file_key_encrypted: String,
    pub file_iv: String,
    pub size: u64,
    pub timestamp: u64,
    pub encryption_mode: String,
}
```

And in `FileMetadata` (lines 174-198):
```rust
#[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
#[serde(default)]
pub versions: Option<Vec<VersionEntry>>,
```

**Cross-platform parity:** Both TypeScript and Rust use `#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]` to produce identical JSON serialization.

---

### 4. How `unpinVersionCids` Works in Bin Service

**Location:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts:759-775`

```typescript
async function unpinVersionCids(fileMeta: {
  versions?: Array<{ cid?: string; size?: number }>;
}): Promise<void> {
  if (!Array.isArray(fileMeta.versions)) return;
  for (const version of fileMeta.versions) {
    try {
      if (version.cid) {
        await unpinFromIpfs(version.cid);
      }
      if (Number.isFinite(version.size)) {
        useQuotaStore.getState().removeUsage(version.size!);
      }
    } catch {
      // Best-effort for individual version cleanup
    }
  }
}
```

**Key points:**
- Called from `unpinFileCids()` after unpinning the current file content
- Also called from `cleanupFolderCids()` for each nested file
- Best-effort cleanup — individual version failures don't block the overall bin operation
- **Quota is reclaimed for each version's size**
- Uses loose duck-typing (only checks for `cid` and `size` properties)

---

### 5. Versions in `unpinFileCids` Permanent Delete Flow

**Location:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts:696-753`

The function has two paths:

**Path 1: Preferred (with `contentCid` captured at soft-delete time)**
```typescript
if (entry.contentCid) {
  await unpinFromIpfs(entry.contentCid);
  if (Number.isFinite(entry.contentSize)) {
    useQuotaStore.getState().removeUsage(entry.contentSize!);
  }
  // Also unpin the file metadata CID and any version CIDs
  if (entry.filePointer) {
    const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(entry.filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName);
    if (resolved?.cid) {
      // Best-effort: try to parse file metadata for version CIDs.
      // This uses plaintext JSON parsing — if the metadata is encrypted
      // (which it is for v2 entries), version cleanup is skipped here
      // but will succeed for folder-nested files in cleanupFolderCids.
      try {
        const metaBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
        const metaJson = new TextDecoder().decode(metaBytes);
        const fileMeta = JSON.parse(metaJson);
        await unpinVersionCids(fileMeta);
      } catch {
        // Expected for encrypted metadata — version cleanup is best-effort
      }
      await unpinFromIpfs(resolved.cid).catch(() => {});
    }
  }
  return;
}
```

**Path 2: Legacy fallback (without `contentCid`)**
```typescript
if (!entry.filePointer) return;
const fileIpnsName = entry.filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName;
const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(fileIpnsName);
if (!resolved?.cid) return;

const metaBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
const metaJson = new TextDecoder().decode(metaBytes);
const fileMeta = JSON.parse(metaJson);

if (fileMeta.cid) {
  await unpinFromIpfs(fileMeta.cid);
  if (fileMeta.size && typeof fileMeta.size === 'number') {
    useQuotaStore.getState().removeUsage(fileMeta.size);
  }
}
await unpinVersionCids(fileMeta);  // <-- versions cleanup here
```

---

### 6. Versions in `cleanupFolderCids` (Recursive Folder Delete)

**Location:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts:785-846`

When permanently deleting a folder, the function:
1. Unwraps the folder's AES key (ECIES)
2. Resolves folder IPNS to get metadata CID
3. Decrypts folder metadata with the unwrapped key
4. For each child file:
   ```typescript
   const fileMeta = await decryptFileMetadata(encryptedFileMeta, folderKey);
   if (fileMeta.cid) {
     await unpinFromIpfs(fileMeta.cid);
     if (Number.isFinite(fileMeta.size)) {
       useQuotaStore.getState().removeUsage(fileMeta.size);
     }
   }
   // Unpin any historical version CIDs
   await unpinVersionCids(fileMeta);  // <-- versions cleanup here
   ```
5. Recurses for subfolders with the user's private key

**Critical difference:** In `cleanupFolderCids`, file metadata is **decrypted** so version CIDs are always accessible. In `unpinFileCids` for standalone files, metadata may be encrypted, so version cleanup is best-effort.

---

### 7. Data Flow Summary

```
Permanent Delete Bin Entry
    ↓
cleanupEntryCids() [dispatcher]
    ├─→ File entry
    │   └─→ unpinFileCids()
    │       ├─→ Unpin current content CID
    │       ├─→ Unpin file metadata IPNS CID
    │       └─→ unpinVersionCids()
    │           └─→ Loop over versions[], unpin each version.cid
    │               └─→ Quota reduction per version.size
    │
    └─→ Folder entry
        └─→ cleanupFolderCids() [recursive]
            ├─→ Decrypt folder metadata with unwrapped folderKey
            ├─→ For each file child:
            │   ├─→ Decrypt file metadata
            │   ├─→ Unpin content CID
            │   ├─→ Quota reduction
            │   └─→ unpinVersionCids()
            │       └─→ All versions unpinned + quota reclaimed
            └─→ For each subfolder child:
                └─→ Recurse with user privateKey
```

---

### 8. Key Observations

**Metadata Encryption Implication:**
- **v2 FileMetadata is encrypted** with the parent folder's `folderKey`
- When a file is in the bin, its metadata may be encrypted, so standalone decryption fails
- The workaround: `unpinFileCids` uses plaintext JSON parsing and skips on error
- **Resolution:** For folder-nested files (common case), `cleanupFolderCids` decrypts them properly and unpins versions correctly
- For truly standalone bin entries (rare), version cleanup is best-effort

**Quota Accounting:**
- Each VersionEntry contributes its `size` to user quota
- Permanent delete reclaims all: current content + all historical versions
- Quota changes are **immediate** (fires `useQuotaStore.removeUsage()` per CID)

**CID Cleanup Breadth:**
- **File content CID** (current version)
- **File metadata IPNS CID** (contains all versions)
- **Version content CIDs** (each entry in `versions` array)
- **Folder metadata CID** (for folder entries)

---

### 9. No Rust Bin Implementation (Yet)

The `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/bin.rs` file contains only **type definitions** — `RecycleBinMetadata`, `BinEntry`, `BinItemType`. The actual permanent-delete and version cleanup logic is **only in TypeScript** (`apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`). Desktop app would delegate bin operations to the web API or replicate this logic when Rust implementation is needed.

---

This analysis captures the complete versioning picture: VersionEntry is a self-contained snapshot with all crypto material needed for independent decryption, and the permanent-delete flow systematically unpins all versions while reclaiming quota, with special handling for encrypted metadata in standalone files vs. decrypted metadata in recursive folder cleanup.

## COMPREHENSIVE E2E TEST STRUCTURE ANALYSIS FOR BIN/RECYCLE-BIN

Based on a thorough examination of the test codebase, here's the complete structure and patterns for E2E testing:

### 1. **MAIN BIN TEST FILE**

**Location:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/e2e/tests/recycle-bin.spec.ts`

**Structure:** Uses `test.describe.serial()` for sequential test execution with shared browser context
- **Test Setup:**
  - Single `beforeAll` hook that logs in once and initializes all page objects
  - Single `afterAll` hook for cleanup (closes context, deletes test files)
  - Uses unique `runId = Date.now().toString()` to avoid naming collisions

- **Key Test Cases (7 total):**
  - **TC01**: Delete file moves to bin
  - **TC02**: Restore file from bin back to files
  - **TC03**: Permanently delete from bin
  - **TC04**: Empty bin removes all items
  - **TC05**: Bin item displays metadata (name, time remaining)
  - **TC06**: Sidebar navigation to bin page works
  - **TC07**: Permanent delete reclaims storage without crash (GAP-1 fix)

- **Test Pattern Each Test Follows:**
  1. Create test file with unique name
  2. Upload via upload zone
  3. Wait for file to appear in list (60s timeout)
  4. Perform action (delete, restore, etc.)
  5. Wait for state change (disappear from list, appear in bin, etc.)
  6. Navigate as needed
  7. Verify results

---

### 2. **PAGE OBJECTS (View Layer Abstraction)**

#### **BinPage** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/e2e/page-objects/pages/bin.page.ts`)

Primary interface for bin interactions:

```typescript
// Navigation
binNavItem(): Locator
async navigate(): Promise<void>

// List access
binList(): Locator
emptyState(): Locator
binItems(): Locator
async getBinItemCount(): Promise<number>
getBinItemByName(name: string): Locator
async waitForBinItem(name: string, options?: { timeout?: number }): Promise<void>
async waitForBinItemToDisappear(name: string, options?: { timeout?: number }): Promise<void>

// Actions (composite)
async restoreItem(name: string): Promise<void>
async permanentlyDeleteItem(name: string): Promise<void>
async emptyBin(): Promise<void>

// Context menu
async rightClickItem(name: string): Promise<void>
async clickContextRestore(): Promise<void>
async clickContextPermanentDelete(): Promise<void>

// Empty bin button

```

**Selector Strategy:**
- `data-testid` for structural elements: `nav-item-bin`, `bin-list`, `bin-item-*`, `bin-context-menu`, `empty-bin-btn`, `bin-empty-state`
- Class selectors: `.dialog-button--destructive` for confirm dialogs
- Role selectors: `button[role="menuitem"]` for menu options

### 3. **TEST UTILITIES**

#### **Web3Auth Helpers** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/e2e/utils/web3auth-helpers.ts`)

Two authentication paths:

1. **Full Web3Auth Flow** (`loginViaEmail()`):
   - Navigate to login
   - Enter email
   - Click send OTP
   - Wait for OTP input
   - Enter OTP code
   - Click verify
   - Wait for `/files` redirect (60s)

2. **Test Endpoint (TEST_LOGIN_SECRET)** (`loginViaTestEndpoint()`):
   - POST `/auth/test-login` with email + secret
   - Handles vault init/load
   - Injects state into Zustand stores via `page.evaluate()`
   - Returns `TestAuthState` object

**Page reload recovery:**
```typescript
await reinjectTestAuthAfterReload(page, state?)
```
Refreshes token and re-injects state after reload.

#### **Conflict Detection Helpers** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/e2e/utils/conflict-helpers.ts`)

  page: Page;

  accessToken: string;
  rootIpnsName: string;
}): Promise<{ newSequenceNumber: string }>
```

This simulates another device publishing by calling the API publish endpoint directly. Shows pattern for API-level test helpers beyond UI.

### 5. **QUOTA/STORAGE TESTING PATTERN**

From **TC07** (GAP-1 fix test):

```typescript
test('TC07: permanent delete reclaims storage without crash', async () => {
  const fileName = `bin-perm-${runId}.txt`;
  const fileContent = 'x'.repeat(1024); // 1KB file for measurable quota change

  // 1. Upload 1KB file
  const testFile = createTestTextFile(fileName, fileContent);
  await uploadZone.uploadFile(testFile.path);
  await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(fileName, { timeout: 60000 });
  await page.waitForTimeout(3000); // Wait for upload to propagate

  // 2. Delete (soft-delete)
  await fileList.rightClickItem(fileName);
  await contextMenu.waitForOpen();
  await contextMenu.clickDelete();
  await confirmDialog.waitForOpen();
  await confirmDialog.clickConfirm();
  await fileList.waitForItemToDisappear(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });

  // 3. Navigate to bin and permanently delete
  await binPage.navigate();
  await binPage.waitForBinItem(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });
  await binPage.permanentlyDeleteItem(fileName);

  // 4. PRIMARY ASSERTION: Permanent delete completes (no crash)
  await binPage.waitForBinItemToDisappear(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });

  // 5. SECONDARY ASSERTION (fragile): Check quota via Zustand store
  const quotaDecreased = await page.evaluate(() => {
    try {
      const store = (window as any).__ZUSTAND_STORES__?.quota;
      if (!store) return null;
      const state = store.getState();
      return typeof state.usedBytes === 'number';
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  });
  if (quotaDecreased !== null) {
    expect(quotaDecreased).toBe(true);
  }
});
```

**Key Insights:**
- **Quota verification is fragile** — `window.__ZUSTAND_STORES__` may not be exposed in CI
- **Primary assertion** is that the operation completes without crash
- **Secondary assertion** checks store functionality if accessible
- **Wait timeouts** are important (3s post-upload, 30s for state changes, 60s for UI visibility)

---

### 6. **TESTING PATTERNS & BEST PRACTICES**

#### **Serial Test Execution**
- Use `test.describe.serial()` for tests that depend on shared state
- Single `beforeAll` for login (60s timeout), `afterAll` for cleanup
- All page objects initialized in `beforeAll`

#### **Waiting & Timeouts**
- **UI appearance:** 60s for upload, 30s for deletion/state changes
- **Post-action delays:** 3s after upload for propagation
- **Dialog operations:** 5s for context menu, immediate for dialogs (already visible)

#### **Test Data Isolation**
- Use `Date.now()` for unique file names (prevents collisions across parallel runs)
- Create test files in `fixtures/files/` directory
- Always call `cleanupTestFiles()` in `afterAll`

#### **Page Objects Benefits**
- Centralize selectors (CSS, data-testid, role-based)
- Composite actions (e.g., `permanentlyDeleteItem()` = right-click + click menu + confirm)
- Reusable across multiple tests

#### **State Management**
- Tests can access Zustand stores via `page.evaluate()` in dev mode
- Useful for verification of internal state (quota, cache, etc.)
- Not reliable in CI (stores may not be exposed)

#### **Action Sequencing**
```typescript
// Pattern: Action → Wait for intermediary → Continue
await fileList.rightClickItem(fileName);       // Trigger
await contextMenu.waitForOpen();                // Wait for open
await contextMenu.clickDelete();                // Click option
await confirmDialog.waitForOpen();              // Wait for dialog
await confirmDialog.clickConfirm();             // Confirm
await fileList.waitForItemToDisappear(fileName); // Wait for result
```

---

### 7. **KEY SELECTORS USED IN BIN TESTS**

| Element | Selector | Method |
|---------|----------|--------|
| Bin nav | `[data-testid="nav-item-bin"]` | `page.getByTestId()` |
| Bin list | `[data-testid="bin-list"]` | `page.getByTestId()` |
| Bin item | `[data-testid^="bin-item-"]` | `page.locator()` with hasText |
| Empty state | `[data-testid="bin-empty-state"]` | `page.getByTestId()` |
| Confirm button | `.dialog-button--destructive` | `page.locator()` |
| Context menu | `[data-testid="bin-context-menu"]` | `page.getByTestId()` |
| Menu items | `button[role="menuitem"]` with hasText | Role + text match |

---

### 8. **IMPORTANT TEST BEHAVIOR PATTERNS**

#### **Error Handling in Tests**
- No try-catch blocks (fail fast)
- Use `waitFor({ state: 'visible' })` with timeouts instead of polling
- Tests marked `test.slow()` for slow operations (allow extra time)

#### **Assertions**
- Use Playwright's `expect()` from `@playwright/test`
- Check visibility: `await expect(element).toBeVisible()`
- Check text: `await expect(element).toContainText(text)`
- Match patterns: `toMatch(/regex/)`

#### **Navigation**
- Use sidebar nav item clicks (not URL manipulation)
- Wait for URL change: `page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 15000 })`
- Allow 15s for nav, 60s for login

#### **File Operations Lifecycle**
1. **Upload:** Click dropzone → `setInputFiles()` → wait for file to appear in list
2. **Soft Delete:** Right-click → menu click → confirm → wait for disappear
3. **Bin:** Navigate → list appears with deleted items
4. **Restore:** Right-click bin item → restore → item disappears from bin
5. **Permanent Delete:** Right-click bin item → delete permanently → confirm → disappear
6. **Empty Bin:** Click button → confirm → empty state appears

---

### 9. **FOR VERSION-RELATED PERMANENT DELETE TESTS**

Based on the existing patterns, here's what would be needed:

**Test Structure:**
```typescript
test('version-related permanent delete test', async () => {
  test.slow();
  const fileName = `version-test-${runId}.txt`;
  
  // 1. Upload initial version
  const testFile = createTestTextFile(fileName, 'Version 1 content');
  await uploadZone.uploadFile(testFile.path);
  await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(fileName, { timeout: 60000 });
  
  // 2. Delete (moves to bin)
  await fileList.rightClickItem(fileName);
  await contextMenu.waitForOpen();
  await contextMenu.clickDelete();
  await confirmDialog.waitForOpen();
  await confirmDialog.clickConfirm();
  await fileList.waitForItemToDisappear(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });
  
  // 3. Navigate to bin
  await binPage.navigate();
  await binPage.waitForBinItem(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });
  
  // 4. Verify bin item metadata (version info, if displayed)
  const binItem = binPage.getBinItemByName(fileName);
  await expect(binItem).toContainText(fileName);
  
  // 5. Permanently delete
  await binPage.permanentlyDeleteItem(fileName);
  
  // 6. Assert item gone from bin
  await binPage.waitForBinItemToDisappear(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });
  
  // 7. Verify version data cleaned up (if accessible via Zustand)
  const versionsCleared = await page.evaluate(() => {
    try {
      const store = (window as any).__ZUSTAND_STORES__?.versions;
      if (!store) return null;
      const state = store.getState();
      return typeof state.versions === 'object';
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  });
  if (versionsCleared !== null) {
    expect(typeof versionsCleared).toBe('boolean');
  }
});
```

**Key Considerations:**
- Reuse existing page objects and selectors
- Follow timeout patterns (60s upload, 30s operations)
- Use `runId` for unique names
- Test primary path (deletion completes), secondary verification (state cleanup) optional
- Use Zustand store access for version metadata verification (if exposed)
- Follow serial test structure with single beforeAll/afterAll

---

This comprehensive analysis provides the complete blueprint for understanding and extending the E2E test suite with version-related permanent delete tests.

## Summary: Desktop Rust Bin/Recycle-Bin Test Structure Analysis

Based on thorough exploration of the CipherBox desktop application codebase, here's a comprehensive guide to the bin/recycle-bin functionality and testing patterns:

### 1. **Core Bin Structures** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/bin.rs`)

**RecycleBinMetadata** - Top-level structure:
```rust
pub struct RecycleBinMetadata {
    pub version: String,           // Always "v1"
    pub sequence_number: u64,      // Monotonic update counter
    pub entries: Vec<BinEntry>,    // List of deleted items
}
```

**BinEntry** - Individual bin item:
```rust
pub struct BinEntry {
    pub id: String,
    pub item_type: BinItemType,    // File or Folder
    pub name: String,
    pub original_parent_ipns_name: String,
    pub original_path: String,
    pub deleted_at: u64,           // Unix ms
    pub size: u64,
    pub mime_type: String,
    pub content_cid: Option<String>,           // Optional: file content CID
    pub content_size: Option<u64>,
    pub file_pointer: Option<FilePointer>,     // For files
    pub folder_entry: Option<FolderEntry>,    // For folders
}
```

**BinItemType** - Enum:
```rust
pub enum BinItemType {
    File,
    Folder,
}
```

### 2. **Version-Related Structures** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/folder.rs`)

**VersionEntry** - Individual file version:
```rust
pub struct VersionEntry {
    pub cid: String,                           // IPFS CID of encrypted content
    pub file_key_encrypted: String,            // Hex-encoded ECIES-wrapped AES-256 key
    pub file_iv: String,                       // Hex-encoded IV
    pub size: u64,                             // Original file size
    pub timestamp: u64,                        // Unix ms when version created
    pub encryption_mode: String,               // "GCM" or "CTR"
}
```

**FileMetadata** - Per-file metadata (stored in file's own IPNS record):
```rust
pub struct FileMetadata {
    pub version: String,
    pub cid: String,                           // Current file CID
    pub file_key_encrypted: String,
    pub file_iv: String,
    pub size: u64,
    pub mime_type: String,
    pub encryption_mode: String,
    pub created_at: u64,
    pub modified_at: u64,
    pub versions: Option<Vec<VersionEntry>>,  // Past versions (newest first)
}
```

### 3. **Version Management Logic** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/read_ops.rs`)

**Key Constants** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/constants.rs`):
- `MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE: usize = 10`
- `VERSION_COOLDOWN_MS: u64 = 15 * 60 * 1000` (15 minutes)

**Version Pruning Logic** (lines 583-617 in read_ops.rs):
```rust
let (new_versions, pruned_cids) = if should_version {
    // Create new version entry from old file metadata
    let version_entry = VersionEntry {
        cid: old_c.clone(),
        file_key_encrypted: old_encrypted_key.clone(),
        file_iv: old_iv.clone(),
        size: old_size,
        timestamp: now_ms,
        encryption_mode: old_mode.clone(),
    };
    
    // Prepend to existing versions
    let mut versions = vec![version_entry];
    versions.extend(existing_versions.unwrap_or_default());
    
    // Prune excess versions
    let pruned: Vec<String> = if versions.len() > MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE {
        versions.split_off(MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE)
            .into_iter()
            .map(|v| v.cid)
            .collect()
    } else {
        vec![]
    };
    (Some(versions), pruned)
};
```

**UploadComplete Struct** (fuse/mod.rs lines 96-106):
```rust
pub struct UploadComplete {
    pub ino: u64,
    pub new_cid: String,
    pub parent_ino: u64,
    pub old_file_cid: Option<String>,
    pub pruned_cids: Vec<String>,        // CIDs to unpin
    pub write_generation: u64,
}
```

### 5. **Test Helper Utilities** (`/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/utils.rs`)

```rust
pub fn generate_file_key() -> [u8; 32]      // Random AES-256 key
pub fn generate_iv() -> [u8; 12]            // Random 12-byte IV
pub fn generate_uuid_v4() -> String         // UUID v4 format
pub fn mime_from_extension(filename: &str) -> &'static str

```

### 6. **Encryption/Decryption Functions** (bin.rs)

```rust
pub fn encrypt_bin_metadata(
    metadata: &RecycleBinMetadata,
    user_public_key: &[u8],
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, BinError>

pub fn decrypt_bin_metadata(
    ciphertext: &[u8],
    user_private_key: &[u8],
) -> Result<RecycleBinMetadata, BinError>

pub fn empty_bin_metadata() -> RecycleBinMetadata

// Also: encrypt_file_metadata, decrypt_file_metadata in folder.rs
pub fn encrypt_file_metadata(
    metadata: &FileMetadata,
    folder_key: &[u8; 32],
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, FolderError>

pub fn decrypt_file_metadata(
    sealed: &[u8],
    folder_key: &[u8; 32],
) -> Result<FileMetadata, FolderError>
```

### 7. **Error Types**

**BinError** (bin.rs):
- `EncryptionFailed`
- `DecryptionFailed`
- `SerializationFailed`
- `DeserializationFailed`
- `ValidationFailed(String)`

**FolderError** (folder.rs):
- `EncryptionFailed(AesError)`
- `SerializationFailed`
- `DeserializationFailed`

### 8. **Test Pattern Conventions**

1. **Naming**: `test_<feature>_<scenario>` or `<struct>_<operation>_<condition>`
2. **ECIES tests use**: Pre-generated test keys from TypeScript (for cross-language verification)
3. **File metadata tests**:
   - Create a `FileMetadata` struct directly
   - Call `encrypt_file_metadata` with `utils::generate_file_key()`
   - Call `decrypt_file_metadata` and assert equality
4. **Bin tests**:
   - Create `RecycleBinMetadata` with `bin::empty_bin_metadata()` or direct construction
   - Decode test keys with `hex::decode(CONSTANT).unwrap()`
   - Use `make_test_bin_entry` helper for consistent test data
   - Test JSON serialization with `serde_json::to_string()`
   - Test JSON deserialization with `serde_json::from_str()`

### 9. **Key Patterns for Version Testing**

For `unpinVersionCids` or version-related unit tests, follow these patterns:

1. **Create a FileMetadata with versions**:
```rust
let metadata = FileMetadata {
    version: "v1".to_string(),
    cid: "bafy_current".to_string(),
    file_key_encrypted: "key_hex".to_string(),
    file_iv: "iv_hex".to_string(),
    size: 1000,
    mime_type: "text/plain".to_string(),
    encryption_mode: "GCM".to_string(),
    created_at: now_ms,
    modified_at: now_ms,
    versions: Some(vec![
        VersionEntry {
            cid: "bafy_v1".to_string(),
            file_key_encrypted: "key1".to_string(),
            file_iv: "iv1".to_string(),
            size: 800,
            timestamp: now_ms - 1000,
            encryption_mode: "GCM".to_string(),
        },
        VersionEntry {
            cid: "bafy_v2".to_string(),
            file_key_encrypted: "key2".to_string(),
            file_iv: "iv2".to_string(),
            size: 900,
            timestamp: now_ms - 2000,
            encryption_mode: "GCM".to_string(),
        },
    ]),
};
```

2. **Test version pruning logic**:
```rust
#[test]
fn versions_exceed_max_returns_pruned_cids() {
    // Create FileMetadata with MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE + 5 versions
    // Verify that excess versions are identified for unpinning
    // Assert pruned_cids contains only the overflow CIDs
}
```

3. **Test version ordering** (newest first):
```rust
#[test]
fn version_list_maintains_newest_first_order() {
    // Create versions with known timestamps
    // Assert versions[0].timestamp > versions[1].timestamp > ...
}
```

4. **Test cooldown logic**:
```rust
#[test]
fn version_creation_respects_cooldown_period() {
    // Create FileMetadata with last version timestamp
    // Assert that new versions aren't created within VERSION_COOLDOWN_MS
}
```

page.evaluate()\n\n### Desktop (Rust) side\n- `VersionEntry` struct in `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/folder.rs` (lines 148-166)\n- `FileMetadata` struct has `versions: Option<Vec<VersionEntry>>` field\n- Existing tests in `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/tests.rs` cover bin metadata encrypt/decrypt roundtrips and FileMetadata serialization\n- `encrypt_file_metadata` and `decrypt_file_metadata` exist in `folder.rs`\n- Test helper `make_test_bin_entry` creates bin entries for testing\n- ECIES test keys: `ECIES_TEST_PRIVATE_KEY` and `ECIES_TEST_PUBLIC_KEY` constants\n- There is NO `unpinVersionCids` or permanent-delete logic in Rust — only types and serialization\n\n## Key question\n\nSince the web `unpinVersionCids` is a runtime function (not pure), and the desktop has no permanent-delete logic, what kind of tests make sense?\n\nFor **web**: Unit-style tests that mock `unpinFromIpfs` and `useQuotaStore` would be ideal, but the function is a private module-level function in `bin.service.ts`. A Playwright E2E test could test the full flow but can't create versioned files since versioning happens via FUSE. The most practical approach is probably a **unit test** that tests the version cleanup logic, but we'd need to either export the function or test it indirectly.\n\nFor **desktop Rust**: We can test that `FileMetadata` with versions serializes/deserializes correctly (proving the data is preserved through encrypt/decrypt), and that `BinEntry` with version-containing file metadata works correctly. This ensures that when permanent-delete logic is eventually implemented in Rust, the data structures are fully tested.\n\n## What I'd like you to plan\n\nDesign the specific test cases and implementation approach for both web and desktop, considering:\n1. What's actually testable given the architecture\n2. The existing test patterns (Playwright for web E2E, Rust #[test] for desktop)\n3. Whether we need to export/restructure anything to make functions testable\n4. The most valuable tests that prove versions are handled correctly during permanent delete\n\nReference the existing test patterns from:\n- `tests/e2e/tests/recycle-bin.spec.ts` (7 existing test cases, serial execution, page objects)\n- `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/tests.rs` (bin metadata roundtrips, FileMetadata roundtrips)\n- Test helpers in `tests/e2e/utils/test-files.ts`\n- `make_test_bin_entry` helper in crypto/tests.rs"
}

     1→/**
     2→ * @cipherbox/crypto - Recycle Bin Tests
     3→ *
     4→ * Tests for bin crypto operations: IPNS derivation, ECIES encryption/decryption,
     5→ * and schema validation.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
     9→import * as secp256k1 from '@noble/secp256k1';
    10→import { deriveBinIpnsKeypair } from '../bin/derive-ipns';
    11→import { encryptBinMetadata, decryptBinMetadata } from '../bin/encrypt';
    12→import { validateBinMetadata } from '../bin/schema';
    13→import type { RecycleBinMetadata } from '../bin/types';
    14→
    15→/**
    16→ * Generate a secp256k1 keypair for testing.
    17→ */
    18→function generateTestKeypair(): { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array } {
    19→  const privateKey = secp256k1.utils.randomPrivateKey();
    20→  const publicKey = secp256k1.getPublicKey(privateKey, false); // uncompressed, 65 bytes
    21→  return { publicKey, privateKey };
    22→}
    23→
    24→/**
    25→ * Create a minimal valid RecycleBinMetadata for testing.
    26→ */
    27→function createTestBinMetadata(entryCount = 1): RecycleBinMetadata {
    28→  const now = Date.now();
    29→  const entries = Array.from({ length: entryCount }, (_, i) => ({
    30→    id: `entry-${i}-${crypto.randomUUID()}`,
    31→    itemType: i % 2 === 0 ? ('file' as const) : ('folder' as const),
    32→    name: `test-item-${i}.txt`,
    33→    originalParentIpnsName: `k51${'a'.repeat(59)}`,
    34→    originalPath: `My Vault / Documents / test-item-${i}.txt`,
    35→    deletedAt: now - i * 60_000,
    36→    size: i * 1024,
    37→    mimeType: i % 2 === 0 ? 'text/plain' : '',
    38→    ...(i % 2 === 0
    39→      ? {
    40→          filePointer: {
    41→            type: 'file' as const,
    42→            id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    43→            name: `test-item-${i}.txt`,
    44→            fileMetaIpnsName: `k51${'c'.repeat(59)}`,
    45→            createdAt: now - i * 120_000,
    46→            modifiedAt: now - i * 60_000,
    47→          },
    48→        }
    49→      : {
    50→          folderEntry: {
    51→            type: 'folder' as const,
    52→            id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    53→            name: `test-item-${i}.txt`,
    54→            ipnsName: `k51${'b'.repeat(59)}`,
    55→            ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: `${'dd'.repeat(48)}`,
    56→            folderKeyEncrypted: `${'ee'.repeat(48)}`,
    57→            createdAt: now - i * 120_000,
    58→            modifiedAt: now - i * 60_000,
    59→          },
    60→        }),
    61→  }));
    62→
    63→  return {
    64→    version: 'v1',
    65→    sequenceNumber: entryCount,
    66→    entries,
    67→  };
    68→}
    69→
    70→// ─── IPNS Derivation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    71→
    72→describe('deriveBinIpnsKeypair', () => {
    73→  it('derives an IPNS name starting with k51 from a 32-byte key', async () => {
    74→    const keypair = generateTestKeypair();
    75→    const result = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(keypair.privateKey);
    76→
    77→    expect(result.ipnsName).toMatch(/^k51/);
    78→    expect(result.privateKey).toBeInstanceOf(Uint8Array);
    79→    expect(result.privateKey.length).toBe(32);
    80→    expect(result.publicKey).toBeInstanceOf(Uint8Array);
    81→    expect(result.publicKey.length).toBe(32);
    82→  });
    83→
    84→  it('produces the same IPNS name for the same privateKey (determinism)', async () => {
    85→    const keypair = generateTestKeypair();
    86→
    87→    const result1 = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(keypair.privateKey);
    88→    const result2 = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(keypair.privateKey);
    89→
    90→    expect(result1.ipnsName).toBe(result2.ipnsName);
    91→    expect(result1.privateKey).toEqual(result2.privateKey);
    92→    expect(result1.publicKey).toEqual(result2.publicKey);
    93→  });
    94→
    95→  it('produces different IPNS names for different privateKeys', async () => {
    96→    const keypair1 = generateTestKeypair();
    97→    const keypair2 = generateTestKeypair();
    98→
    99→    const result1 = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(keypair1.privateKey);
   100→    const result2 = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(keypair2.privateKey);

   103→  });

   108→  });
   109→});

     1→/**
     2→ * Upload Error Recovery Tests
     3→ *

```
     1→# Fix: Codecov Base Upload workflow 404
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→The "Codecov Base Upload" workflow (`codecov-base.yml`) fails every time it runs on push to `main`. The step "Download coverage from latest CI run" gets a 404 from the GitHub API. This means Codecov never gets base branch coverage, so PR coverage diffs don't work.
     5→
     6→## Root Cause
     7→In `.github/workflows/codecov-base.yml` line 27-29, the `gh api` call uses `-f per_page=5`. The `-f` flag causes `gh api` to switch from GET to POST. The GitHub API endpoint (`/actions/workflows/{id}/runs`) only accepts GET, returning 404 for POST.
     8→
     9→## Fix
    10→**File:** `.github/workflows/codecov-base.yml`
    11→
    12→Change line 27-29 from:
    13→```bash
    14→RUN_ID=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/workflows/ci.yml/runs \
    15→  --jq '[.workflow_runs[] | select(.conclusion == "success")][0].id' \
    16→  -f per_page=5)
    17→```
    18→To:
    19→```bash
    20→RUN_ID=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/workflows/ci.yml/runs?per_page=5" \
    21→  --jq '[.workflow_runs[] | select(.conclusion == "success")][0].id')
    22→```
    23→
    24→No other `gh api` calls in the file use `-f`, so this is the only line that needs changing.
    25→
    26→## Verification
    27→- Push the fix to a branch, merge to `main`, and confirm the Codecov Base Upload workflow passes
    28→- Or: manually run the corrected `gh api` command to confirm it returns a valid run ID (already verified locally)
    29→
```

Follow the pattern in `bin.test.ts` (vitest, `describe`/`it`, helper functions).\n\n### Test cases (~7 tests)\n\n**`describe('encryptFileMetadata / decryptFileMetadata')`**\n1. Round-trip with no versions — baseline, `versions` undefined\n2. Round-trip preserves versions array — 2-3 `VersionEntry` objects, assert all fields (`cid`, `fileKeyEncrypted`, `fileIv`, `size`, `timestamp`, `encryptionMode`) survive\n3. Round-trip with empty versions — `versions: []` preserved (not collapsed to undefined)\n4. Wrong key fails decryption — different `generateFileKey()` for encrypt vs decrypt\n\n**`describe('validateFileMetadata')`**\n5. Accepts metadata with versions — raw object with valid `versions` array passes\n6. Rejects invalid version entry — missing `cid` or non-number `size` throws `CryptoError`\n7. Accepts metadata without versions — `versions` field omitted entirely passes\n\n### Helper function\n```typescript\nfunction createTestFileMetadata(versionCount = 0): FileMetadata {\n  const versions: VersionEntry[] = Array.from({ length: versionCount }, (_, i) => ({\n    cid: `bafyversion${i}${'a'.repeat(40)}`,\n    fileKeyEncrypted: 'aa'.repeat(48),\n    fileIv: 'bb'.repeat(12),\n    size: (i + 1) * 1024,\n    timestamp: Date.now() - (i + 1) * 86400000,\n    encryptionMode: i % 2 === 0 ? 'GCM' as const : 'CTR' as const,\n  }));\n  return {\n    version: 'v1',\n    cid: `bafycurrent${'c'.repeat(42)}`,\n    fileKeyEncrypted: 'dd'.repeat(48),\n    fileIv: 'ee'.repeat(12),\n    size: 4096, mimeType: 'text/plain', encryptionMode: 'GCM',\n    createdAt: Date.now() - 1000000, modifiedAt: Date.now(),\n    ...(versionCount > 0 ? { versions } : {}),\n  };\n}\n```\n\n### Key imports\n```typescript\nimport { encryptFileMetadata, decryptFileMetadata, validateFileMetadata,\n  generateFileKey, type FileMetadata, type VersionEntry } from '../index';\n```\n\n## Layer 2: Desktop Rust Tests\n\n**File to modify:** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/tests.rs` (append after existing tests)\n\n### Test cases (~6 tests)\n\n1. **`file_metadata_with_versions_roundtrip`** — Create `FileMetadata` with 2 `VersionEntry` objects. `encrypt_file_metadata` → `decrypt_file_metadata`. Assert camelCase: `fileKeyEncrypted`, `fileIv`, `encryptionMode`. Assert no snake_case.\n\n4. **`file_metadata_versions_none_omitted_in_json`** — `versions: None`. Serialize. Assert `\"versions\"` key NOT present.\n\n5. **`file_metadata_versions_deserialization_from_json`** — Raw JSON string with camelCase versions. Deserialize. ECIES encrypt/decrypt roundtrip. }

   660→ * Replace file content in v2 metadata (content update).
   661→ *
   662→ * THIS IS THE PRIMARY BENEFIT OF PER-FILE IPNS: content updates publish
   663→ * ONLY the file's IPNS record. The folder metadata is NOT touched because
   664→ * the FilePointer still points to the same fileMetaIpnsName, which now
   665→ * resolves to the updated content.
   666→ *
   667→ * @param params.fileId - ID of file to update
   668→ * @param params.fileIpnsRecord - Updated file IPNS record from updateFileMetadata
   669→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder (used only to find the FilePointer)
   670→ * @returns Void -- no folder metadata update needed
   671→ * @throws Error if file not found
   672→ */
   673→export async function replaceFileInFolder(params: {
   674→  fileId: string;
   675→  fileIpnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
   676→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   677→}): Promise<void> {
   678→  // 1. Verify file exists in parent's children
   679→  const fileExists = params.parentFolderState.children.some(
   680→    (c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === params.fileId
   681→  );
   682→
   683→  if (!fileExists) throw new Error('File not found');
   684→
   685→  // 2. Publish ONLY the file IPNS record (folder metadata untouched!)
   686→  // This is the key optimization: content update does NOT modify folder metadata.
   687→  const publishResult = await batchPublishIpnsRecords([
   688→    { ...params.fileIpnsRecord, recordType: 'file' },
   689→  ]);
   690→
   691→  if (publishResult.totalFailed > 0) {
   692→    throw new Error('Failed to publish file IPNS record');
   693→  }
   694→}
   695→
   696→/**
   697→ * Calculate the maximum depth of a folder's subtree.
   698→ *
   699→ * Used to check if moving a folder would exceed the depth limit.
   700→ *
   701→ * @param folderId - Folder ID to calculate subtree depth for
   702→ * @param folders - Current folder tree
   703→ * @returns Maximum depth in the subtree (0 if folder has no children)
   704→ */
   705→export function calculateSubtreeDepth(
   706→  folderId: string,
   707→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
   708→): number {
   709→  const folder = folders[folderId];
   710→  if (!folder) return 0;
   711→
   712→  let maxChildDepth = 0;
   713→  for (const child of folder.children) {
   714→    if (child.type === 'folder') {
   715→      const childDepth = 1 + calculateSubtreeDepth(child.id, folders);
   716→      maxChildDepth = Math.max(maxChildDepth, childDepth);
   717→    }
   718→  }
   719→
   720→  return maxChildDepth;
   721→}
   722→
   723→/**
   724→ * Check if a folder is a descendant of a potential ancestor.
   725→ *
   726→ * Used to prevent moving a folder into itself or its descendants.
   727→ *
   728→ * @param folderId - Folder to check
   729→ * @param potentialAncestorId - Potential ancestor folder ID
   730→ * @param folders - Current folder tree
   731→ * @returns true if folderId is a descendant of potentialAncestorId
   732→ */
   733→export function isDescendantOf(
   734→  folderId: string,
   735→  potentialAncestorId: string,
   736→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
   737→): boolean {
   738→  let currentId: string | null = folderId;
   739→

    19→/**
    20→ * React hook for file version management operations (restore, delete).

    22→ * Returns loading/error state and operation callbacks.

    24→export function useFileVersions() {
    25→  const [state, setState] = useState<FolderOperationState>({
    26→    isLoading: false,
    27→    error: null,
    28→  });
    29→
    30→  /**
    31→   * Restore a previous version of a file.
    32→   *
    33→   * Swaps the current content with a past version. The current content becomes
    34→   * a new version entry (non-destructive). Publishes updated IPNS record and
    35→   * unpins any pruned version CIDs.
    36→   *
    37→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID ('root' or folder UUID)
    38→   * @param fileId - File ID (UUID)
    39→   * @param versionIndex - Index of version to restore (0 = newest past version)
    40→   */
    41→  const handleRestoreVersion = useCallback(
    42→    async (parentId: string, fileId: string, versionIndex: number): Promise<void> => {
    43→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
    44→      try {
    45→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
    46→        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
    47→        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
    48→
    49→        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
    50→          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
    51→        }
    52→
    53→        // Get parent folder state
    54→        const parentFolder =
    55→          parentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[parentId];
    56→
    57→        if (!parentFolder) {
    58→          throw new Error('Parent folder not found or vault not initialized');
    59→        }
    60→
    61→        // Find the FilePointer
    62→        const filePointer = parentFolder.children.find(
    63→          (c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === fileId
    64→        ) as FilePointer | undefined;
    65→
    66→        if (!filePointer) {
    67→          throw new Error('File not found in folder');
    68→        }
    69→
    70→        // Resolve current file metadata from IPNS
    71→        const { metadata: currentMetadata } = await resolveFileMetadata(
    72→          filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
    73→          parentFolder.folderKey
    74→        );
    75→
    76→        // Decrypt the file IPNS private key from FilePointer (or HKDF fallback)
    77→        const { privateKey: fileIpnsPrivateKey, migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } =
    78→          await getFileIpnsPrivateKey(

    80→            auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,

    82→          );
    83→
    84→        let ipnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;

    91→            folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
    92→            currentMetadata,
    93→            versionIndex,
    94→          }));
    95→        } finally {
    96→          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
    97→        }
    98→
    99→        // Publish the IPNS record (folder metadata untouched)
   100→        await folderService.replaceFileInFolder({
   101→          fileId,
   102→          fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
   103→          parentFolderState: parentFolder,
   104→        });
   105→
   106→        // Update local folder state (modifiedAt and migrated IPNS key on FilePointer)
   107→        const updatedChildren = parentFolder.children.map((child) => {
   108→          if (child.type === 'file' && child.id === fileId) {
   109→            return {
   110→              ...child,
   111→              modifiedAt: Date.now(),

   114→                : {}),

   116→          }

   118→        });
   119→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);

   145→        // Refresh quota

   147→
   148→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });

Perfect! Now I have a complete understanding of the upload flow. Let me provide a comprehensive summary of my findings.

## Upload Flow Analysis - Complete Report

I've thoroughly traced the upload flow in the web app to understand where we can intercept the "duplicate name" error and add a "Replace existing file" option. Here's the complete breakdown:

### 1. UPLOAD COMPONENTS (apps/web/src/components/file-browser/)

**UploadZone.tsx** (Lines 1-104)
- Entry point for drag-drop file uploads
- Uses `useDropUpload()` hook to handle file drops
- Pre-checks for file size violations (100MB max)
- Shows error state inline in a dismissible alert (lines 89-101)
- Error display: inline `.upload-zone-error` div with text + dismiss button

**UploadModal.tsx** (Lines 1-242)
- Collapsible upload progress popup (bottom-right corner)
- Maps upload store status to display status (pending/encrypting/uploading/complete/error)
- Shows current file, progress %, and error message (lines 210-214)
- Actions: Cancel (during upload), Close (after completion/error)
- **Key insight:** Error is shown on line 212: `<div className="upload-zone-error" role="alert">{error}</div>`

**UploadItem.tsx** (Lines 1-107)
- Individual file row in upload queue
- Shows file icon, name, progress bar, status text
- Displays error via `getStatusText()` function (line 26 returns error message)
- Has Retry button slot (lines 76-85) but not currently used for duplicate handling

**EmptyState.tsx** (Lines 1-87)
- Shows folder empty state with terminal ASCII art
- Also accepts drag-drop files
- Shows error inline like UploadZone (lines 69-83)

### 2. HOW addFilesToFolder DETECTS DUPLICATES

**folder.service.ts** (Lines 596-657)

```typescript
export async function addFilesToFolder(params: {
  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
  files: Array<{ fileId, name, fileIpnsRecord, ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted }>;
}): Promise<{ filePointers, newSequenceNumber }> {
  // 1. Build set of existing child names for collision detection
  const existingNames = new Set(params.parentFolderState.children.map((c) => c.name));

  // 2. Create FilePointer for each file, checking collisions
  for (const file of params.files) {
    if (existingNames.has(file.name)) {
      throw new Error(`A file with name "${file.name}" already exists`);  // LINE 614
    }
    existingNames.add(file.name);
    // ... create FilePointer ...
  }
  
  // ... batch publish to IPNS ...
}
```

**Key finding:** The error is thrown AFTER encryption is complete but BEFORE folder metadata update. This is the ideal interception point.

### 3. CURRENT ERROR FLOW (useDropUpload)

**useDropUpload.ts** (Lines 26-119)

Flow:
1. **Pre-upload validation** (lines 50-73):
   - Checks folder cache for existing names (lines 51-62)
   - Sets error in upload store if duplicates found
   - Returns false immediately (blocking upload)

2. **Upload phase** (lines 75-95):
   - Calls `upload(files)` → encrypts + uploads to IPFS
   - Calls `addFiles()` hook to register in folder

3. **Error handling** (lines 99-113):
   - If error thrown, calls `useUploadStore.getState().setError(message)`
   - Cleans up orphaned IPFS pins
   - Returns false

**Critical issue:** Pre-upload check on line 50-62 blocks the entire upload if ANY file duplicates exist in the folder cache. This prevents getting to the actual rename/replace choice.

### 4. ERROR DISPLAY (useUploadStore)

**upload.store.ts** (Lines 1-93)

```typescript
type UploadState = {
  status: UploadStatus;  // 'idle' | 'encrypting' | 'uploading' | 'registering' | 'success' | 'error' | 'cancelled'
  error: string | null;
  // ... other state ...
  setError: (error: string) => void;
};

setError: (error) => set({ status: 'error', error, currentFile: null }),
```

**How it's displayed:**
- UploadModal shows error on line 212: `{error}`
- UploadZone shows error on line 91
- Error persists until user clicks dismiss or starts new upload

### 5. FILE UPDATE CAPABILITY (useFileOperations.ts)

**The "replace" operation already exists:** Lines 338-475

```typescript
const handleUpdateFile = useCallback(
  async (parentId, fileData: {
    fileId: string;
    newCid: string;
    newFileKeyEncrypted: string;
    newFileIv: string;
    newSize: number;
    forceVersion?: boolean;  // KEY: forces version creation
  }): Promise<void> => {
    // ... resolves current file metadata ...
    // ... determines whether to create version ...
    const createVersion = shouldCreateVersion(currentMetadata, fileData.forceVersion ?? false);
    // ... publishes updated file IPNS record ...
  }
);
```

This is exposed as `updateFile` in the `useFolder()` hook (line 62).

### 6. CONFLICT HANDLING (folder-helpers.ts)

The system has a **conflict retry mechanism** for 409 errors (concurrent updates):
- `withConflictRetry()` runs operation, catches 409, resyncs folder, retries once
- This is used for `addFileToFolder` (line 111 in useFileOperations.ts)
- Shows a sync banner during resync

**Lesson:** This architecture supports intelligent error recovery patterns.

---

## WHERE TO INTERCEPT FOR "REPLACE FILE" OPTION

### **Recommended Interception Points:**

1. **In useDropUpload.ts (lines 50-62)** - CLIENT SIDE PRE-CHECK
   - Current: Checks folder cache, blocks if duplicates
   - Change: Instead of blocking, allow upload to proceed, but flag which files have duplicates
   - Pass "willDuplicate" info to upload store state

2. **In useFileOperations.ts handleAddFiles (lines 193-322)** - REGISTRATION PHASE
   - Current: `addFilesToFolder()` throws error on duplicate
   - Change: Catch "A file with name X already exists" error
   - Instead of failing, extract the filename and prompt user for action
   - If user chooses "Replace", call `handleUpdateFile()` on the existing file with new encrypted content

3. **New error dialog component** - USER CHOICE
   - Show when duplicate name error caught
   - Options: "Replace existing file" or "Skip this file" or "Rename"
   - For Replace: need to find the existing file's ID to call `updateFile()`

### **Key Technical Requirements:**

1. **File lookup:** When duplicate error occurs, need to find the existing FilePointer:
   - Already have: parent folder, filename
   - Look up: `parentFolder.children.find(c => c.name === duplicateName && c.type === 'file')`

2. **Pass-through data:** Keep encrypted file data (CID, wrappedKey, IV) available in error state:
   - Current flow discards this after `addFiles()` fails
   - Need to store per-file encrypted data temporarily

3. **State management:** Track which files are awaiting user action:
   - Could add to upload store: `pendingActions?: Array<{filename, action: 'replace'|'skip'|'retry'}>`

4. **Version creation:** When replacing, set `forceVersion: true` in `updateFile()` call:
   - This ensures old content saved as version (VER-01)
   - Line 392 in useFileOperations.ts: `shouldCreateVersion(currentMetadata, fileData.forceVersion ?? false)`

### **Error Message Chain:**

```
UploadZone/EmptyState
  ↓ handleDrop calls handleFileDrop
useDropUpload.handleFileDrop
  ↓ calls upload() → addFiles()
useFileOperations.handleAddFiles
  ↓ calls addFilesToFolder()
folderService.addFilesToFolder
  ↓ throws: "A file with name X already exists"
useFileOperations.handleAddFiles catches error
  ↓ setError(message)
useUploadStore.setError()
  ↓ shows in UploadModal or UploadZone

[NEW] Need to intercept here → show dialog → offer "Replace" action
```

---

## SUMMARY TABLE

| Component | File | Purpose | Current Behavior |
|-----------|------|---------|------------------|
| **Pre-upload** | useDropUpload.ts:50-62 | Checks folder cache | Blocks if duplicate found |
| **Encryption** | upload.service.ts:84-138 | Encrypts files | Sequential, tracks progress |
| **Upload to IPFS** | upload.service.ts:53-78 | Stores encrypted bytes | Per-file, with retry logic |
| **Registration** | useFileOperations.ts:181-322 | Adds to folder metadata | Throws on duplicate name |
| **Error display** | UploadModal.tsx:210-214 | Shows error to user | Static text, no actions |
| **File update** | useFileOperations.ts:338-475 | Replaces file content | Has `forceVersion` support |

---

The complete upload flow is now mapped, and the interception point for adding the "Replace existing file" feature is clear. The infrastructure already exists to support it - we just need to add a dialog component and wire the duplicate name error to offer the user a choice between Replace (with versioning), Skip, or Rename.

  "content": "# Versioned File Delete: UI, E2E Tests, and Desktop Unit Tests\n\n## Context\nWe added `unpinVersionCids()` to permanently delete version CIDs and reclaim quota. But:\n- The web app has no UI to create file versions (re-upload with same name throws an error instead of offering \"Replace\")\n- There's no E2E test proving versioned permanent delete frees all quota\n- Desktop Rust tests don't cover FileMetadata with VersionEntry roundtrips\n\nThis plan adds the \"Replace\" flow to the web UI, writes an E2E test exercising it end-to-end, and adds desktop Rust serialization tests.\n\n---\n\n## Part 1: Web UI — Replace Existing File on Duplicate Upload\n\n### What exists today\n- `addFilesToFolder()` in `folder.service.ts:614` throws `\"A file with name X already exists\"`\n- `useDropUpload.ts:50-62` pre-checks folder cache and blocks upload entirely if duplicates found\n- `handleUpdateFile()` in `useFileOperations.ts:338` already supports `forceVersion: true` which pushes current content into the versions array before replacing\n- `shouldCreateVersion()` in `file-metadata.service.ts:243` returns true when `forceVersion` is true\n\n### Changes needed\n\n**1. `apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts`** — Remove the hard block on duplicate names\n- Lines 50-62: Instead of setting error and returning, collect duplicate filenames and continue\n- After upload completes, pass duplicate info through to the registration step\n- New files (no duplicate) register normally; duplicates trigger the replace dialog\n\n**2. New component: `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/ReplaceFileDialog.tsx`**\n- Modal dialog shown when a duplicate filename is detected during upload\n- Shows: \"A file named `{name}` already exists. Replace it? The current version will be saved in version history.\"\n- Buttons: \"Replace\" (calls `updateFile` with `forceVersion: true`) | \"Skip\"\n- If multiple duplicates, handle sequentially or batch\n\n**3. `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts`** — Wire up the replace flow\n- `handleAddFiles` (line 181): Instead of letting `addFilesToFolder` throw on duplicates, split files into new files vs duplicates\n- New files → `addFilesToFolder` as before\n- Duplicates → stash their encrypted data (CID, wrappedKey, IV, size) and surface to UI\n- When user confirms Replace → call `handleUpdateFile()` with `forceVersion: true` and the existing file's ID\n\n**4. `apps/web/src/stores/upload.store.ts`** — Add pending-replace state\n- New state: `pendingReplacements: Array<{ fileName, fileId, parentId, encryptedData }>`\n- New actions: `setPendingReplacements`, `clearPendingReplacements`\n\n### Flow after changes\n```\nUser drops file with existing name\n  → encrypt + upload to IPFS (same as before)\n  → detect duplicate name in folder\n  → show ReplaceFileDialog\n  → user clicks \"Replace\"\n  → handleUpdateFile(parentId, { fileId: existingFile.id, ...encryptedData, forceVersion: true })\n  → old content pushed to versions array, new content becomes current\n  → quota increases by new file size (old content now in versions)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 2: Web E2E Test — Versioned Permanent Delete Frees Quota\n\n**File:** `tests/e2e/tests/recycle-bin.spec.ts` — Add TC08\n\n### Test flow\n```\nTC08: Permanent delete of versioned file reclaims all quota (current + versions)\n\n1. Navigate to bin\n8. Permanently delete the file\n9. Wait for file to disappear from bin\n10. Verify quota decreased by ~3KB (both current content and version CID freed)\n```\n\n### Key assertions\n- **Primary**: Permanent delete completes without crash (same as TC07)\n- **Secondary**: Quota store's `usedBytes` decreased by approximately the combined size of current + versioned content\n- Quota assertions are best-effort (store may not be exposed in CI)\n\n### Page objects needed\n- Existing: `BinPage`, `FileListPage`, `UploadZonePage`, `ContextMenuPage`, `ConfirmDialogPage`\n- New: Need locator for `ReplaceFileDialog` confirm button (use `data-testid=\"replace-file-confirm\"`)\n\n### Test data\n- `createTestTextFile(`version-v1-${runId}.txt`, 'x'.repeat(1024))` — 1KB\n- `createTestTextFile(`version-v2-${runId}.txt`, 'y'.repeat(2048))` — 2KB (rename to match v1 name before upload, or upload with same name)\n\n---\n\n## Part 3: Desktop Rust Unit Tests — FileMetadata + VersionEntry\n\n**File:** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/tests.rs` — Append new tests\n\n### Helper\n```rust\nfn make_test_version_entry(index: u32) -> VersionEntry {\n    VersionEntry {\n        cid: format!(\"bafyversion{}{}\", index, \"a\".repeat(40)),\n        file_key_encrypted: \"aa\".repeat(48),\n        file_iv: \"bb\".repeat(12),\n        size: (index as u64 + 1) * 1024,\n        timestamp: 1700000000000 + (index as u64 * 86400000),\n        encryption_mode: if index % 2 == 0 { \"GCM\".to_string() } else { \"CTR\".to_string() },\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Test cases (6 tests)\n\n1. **`file_metadata_with_versions_roundtrip`** — FileMetadata with 2 VersionEntry objects. `encrypt_file_metadata` → `decrypt_file_metadata`. Assert all version fields survive.\n\n2. **`file_metadata_with_empty_versions_roundtrip`** — `versions: Some(vec![])` survives roundtrip (not collapsed to None).\n\n3. **`file_metadata_versions_camel_case_serialization`** — Serialize to JSON. Assert camelCase: `fileKeyEncrypted`, `fileIv`, `encryptionMode`. No snake_case.\n\n4. **`file_metadata_versions_none_omitted_in_json`** — `versions: None`. Serialize. Assert `\"versions\"` key not present.\n\n5. **`file_metadata_versions_deserialization_from_json`** — Raw JSON string with camelCase versions. Deserialize. Assert all fields parse correctly.\n\n6. **`bin_entry_content_cid_roundtrip`** — BinEntry with `content_cid` and `content_size` set, in RecycleBinMetadata. ECIES encrypt/decrypt roundtrip. ## Approved Plan:
# Versioned File Delete: UI, E2E Tests, and Desktop Unit Tests

## Context
We added `unpinVersionCids()` to permanently delete version CIDs and reclaim quota. But:
- The web app has no UI to create file versions (re-upload with same name throws an error instead of offering "Replace")
- There's no E2E test proving versioned permanent delete frees all quota
- Desktop Rust tests don't cover FileMetadata with VersionEntry roundtrips

This plan adds the "Replace" flow to the web UI, writes an E2E test exercising it end-to-end, and adds desktop Rust serialization tests.

---

## Part 1: Web UI — Replace Existing File on Duplicate Upload

### What exists today
- `addFilesToFolder()` in `folder.service.ts:614` throws `"A file with name X already exists"`
- `useDropUpload.ts:50-62` pre-checks folder cache and blocks upload entirely if duplicates found
- `handleUpdateFile()` in `useFileOperations.ts:338` already supports `forceVersion: true` which pushes current content into the versions array before replacing
- `shouldCreateVersion()` in `file-metadata.service.ts:243` returns true when `forceVersion` is true

### Changes needed

**1. `apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts`** — Remove the hard block on duplicate names
- Lines 50-62: Instead of setting error and returning, collect duplicate filenames and continue
- After upload completes, pass duplicate info through to the registration step
- New files (no duplicate) register normally; duplicates trigger the replace dialog

**2. New component: `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/ReplaceFileDialog.tsx`**
- Modal dialog shown when a duplicate filename is detected during upload
- Shows: "A file named `{name}` already exists. Replace it? The current version will be saved in version history."
- Buttons: "Replace" (calls `updateFile` with `forceVersion: true`) | "Skip"
- If multiple duplicates, handle sequentially or batch

**3. `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts`** — Wire up the replace flow
- `handleAddFiles` (line 181): Instead of letting `addFilesToFolder` throw on duplicates, split files into new files vs duplicates
- New files → `addFilesToFolder` as before
- Duplicates → stash their encrypted data (CID, wrappedKey, IV, size) and surface to UI
- When user confirms Replace → call `handleUpdateFile()` with `forceVersion: true` and the existing file's ID

**4. `apps/web/src/stores/upload.store.ts`** — Add pending-replace state
- New state: `pendingReplacements: Array<{ fileName, fileId, parentId, encryptedData }>`
- New actions: `setPendingReplacements`, `clearPendingReplacements`

### Flow after changes
```
User drops file with existing name
  → encrypt + upload to IPFS (same as before)
  → detect duplicate name in folder
  → show ReplaceFileDialog
  → user clicks "Replace"
  → handleUpdateFile(parentId, { fileId: existingFile.id, ...encryptedData, forceVersion: true })
  → old content pushed to versions array, new content becomes current
  → quota increases by new file size (old content now in versions)
```

---

## Part 2: Web E2E Test — Versioned Permanent Delete Frees Quota

**File:** `tests/e2e/tests/recycle-bin.spec.ts` — Add TC08

### Test flow
```
TC08: Permanent delete of versioned file reclaims all quota (current + versions)

1. Upload a ~1KB text file (version 1)
2. Wait for upload to complete + propagate
3. Re-upload a ~2KB file with the same name → choose "Replace" in dialog
4. Wait for replace to complete
5. Verify quota usage reflects ~3KB total (1KB version + 2KB current)
   (read via window.__ZUSTAND_STORES__?.quota or page.evaluate)
6. Soft-delete the file (right-click → Delete → Confirm)
7. Navigate to bin
8. Permanently delete the file
9. Wait for file to disappear from bin
10. Verify quota decreased by ~3KB (both current content and version CID freed)
```

### Key assertions
- **Primary**: Permanent delete completes without crash (same as TC07)
- **Secondary**: Quota store's `usedBytes` decreased by approximately the combined size of current + versioned content
- Quota assertions are best-effort (store may not be exposed in CI)

### Page objects needed
- Existing: `BinPage`, `FileListPage`, `UploadZonePage`, `ContextMenuPage`, `ConfirmDialogPage`
- New: Need locator for `ReplaceFileDialog` confirm button (use `data-testid="replace-file-confirm"`)

### Test data
- `createTestTextFile(`version-v1-${runId}.txt`, 'x'.repeat(1024))` — 1KB
- `createTestTextFile(`version-v2-${runId}.txt`, 'y'.repeat(2048))` — 2KB (rename to match v1 name before upload, or upload with same name)

---

## Part 3: Desktop Rust Unit Tests — FileMetadata + VersionEntry

**File:** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/tests.rs` — Append new tests

### Helper
```rust
fn make_test_version_entry(index: u32) -> VersionEntry {
    VersionEntry {
        cid: format!("bafyversion{}{}", index, "a".repeat(40)),
        file_key_encrypted: "aa".repeat(48),
        file_iv: "bb".repeat(12),
        size: (index as u64 + 1) * 1024,
        timestamp: 1700000000000 + (index as u64 * 86400000),
        encryption_mode: if index % 2 == 0 { "GCM".to_string() } else { "CTR".to_string() },
    }
}
```

### Test cases (6 tests)

1. **`file_metadata_with_versions_roundtrip`** — FileMetadata with 2 VersionEntry objects. `encrypt_file_metadata` → `decrypt_file_metadata`. Assert all version fields survive.

2. **`file_metadata_with_empty_versions_roundtrip`** — `versions: Some(vec![])` survives roundtrip (not collapsed to None).

3. **`file_metadata_versions_camel_case_serialization`** — Serialize to JSON. Assert camelCase: `fileKeyEncrypted`, `fileIv`, `encryptionMode`. No snake_case.

4. **`file_metadata_versions_none_omitted_in_json`** — `versions: None`. Serialize. Assert `"versions"` key not present.

5. **`file_metadata_versions_deserialization_from_json`** — Raw JSON string with camelCase versions. Deserialize. Assert all fields parse correctly.

6. **`bin_entry_content_cid_roundtrip`** — BinEntry with `content_cid` and `content_size` set, in RecycleBinMetadata. ECIES encrypt/decrypt roundtrip. Proves bin entry data needed for version cleanup survives.

---

## Files Summary

| File | Action | What |
|------|--------|------|
| `apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts` | Modify | Remove hard block on duplicates, surface to UI |
| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts` | Modify | Split new vs duplicate files, wire replace flow |
| `apps/web/src/stores/upload.store.ts` | Modify | Add `pendingReplacements` state |
| `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/ReplaceFileDialog.tsx` | Create | Dialog: Replace / Skip |
| `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/UploadZone.tsx` | Modify | Render ReplaceFileDialog when pending |
| `tests/e2e/tests/recycle-bin.spec.ts` | Modify | Add TC08 |
| `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/tests.rs` | Modify | Add 6 tests + helper |

## Verification

```bash
# Desktop Rust tests
cd apps/desktop/src-tauri && cargo test --features fuse -- file_metadata_with_versions

# TypeScript typecheck
pnpm typecheck

# E2E test (requires API + frontend running)
cd tests/e2e && pnpm exec playwright test tests/recycle-bin.spec.ts

     1→import { useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { useFileUpload } from './useFileUpload';
     3→import { useFolder } from './useFolder';
     4→import { unpinFromIpfs } from '../lib/api/ipfs';
     5→import { useUploadStore } from '../stores/upload.store';
     6→import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';
     7→import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';
     8→import type { UploadedFile } from '../services/upload.service';
     9→
    10→export const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 100 * 1024 * 1024; // 100MB per FILE-01
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Check whether a drag event carries external files from the OS (Finder/Explorer)
    14→ * rather than an internal app drag (which sets application/json).
    15→ */
    16→export function isExternalFileDrag(dataTransfer: DataTransfer): boolean {
    17→  return dataTransfer.types.includes('Files') && !dataTransfer.types.includes('application/json');
    18→}
    19→
    20→/**
    21→ * Hook for handling external file drops (from Finder/Explorer) anywhere in the app.
    22→ *
    23→ * Provides a `handleFileDrop(files, folderId)` callback that validates,
    24→ * encrypts, uploads, and registers files in the target folder.
    25→ */
    26→export function useDropUpload() {
    27→  const { upload, canUpload, isUploading } = useFileUpload();
    28→  const { addFiles } = useFolder();
    29→
    30→  const handleFileDrop = useCallback(
    31→    async (files: File[], folderId: string): Promise<boolean> => {
    32→      // Filter out oversized files
    33→      const oversized = files.filter((f) => f.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE);
    34→      if (oversized.length > 0) {
    35→        useUploadStore
    36→          .getState()
    37→          .setError(`Files exceed 100MB limit: ${oversized.map((f) => f.name).join(', ')}`);
    38→        return false;
    39→      }
    40→
    41→      if (files.length === 0) return false;
    42→
    43→      // Check quota
    44→      const totalSize = files.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.size, 0);
    45→      if (!canUpload(totalSize)) {
    46→        useUploadStore.getState().setError('Not enough storage space for these files');
    47→        return false;
    48→      }
    49→
    50→      // Check for duplicate names in target folder
    51→      const folder = useFolderStore.getState().folders[folderId];
    52→      if (folder) {
    53→        const existingNames = new Set(folder.children.map((c) => c.name));
    54→        const duplicates = files.filter((f) => existingNames.has(f.name));
    55→        if (duplicates.length > 0) {
    56→          useUploadStore
    57→            .getState()
    58→            .setError(
    59→              `File${duplicates.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} already exist${duplicates.length === 1 ? 's' : ''} in this folder: ${duplicates.map((f) => f.name).join(', ')}`
    60→            );
    61→          return false;
    62→        }
    63→      }
    64→
    65→      // Check for duplicates within batch (independent of folder cache)
    66→      const batchNames = new Set<string>();
    67→      for (const f of files) {
    68→        if (batchNames.has(f.name)) {
    69→          useUploadStore.getState().setError(`Duplicate file name in selection: ${f.name}`);
    70→          return false;
    71→        }
    72→        batchNames.add(f.name);
    73→      }
    74→
    75→      let uploadedFiles: UploadedFile[] | undefined;
    76→      try {
    77→        uploadedFiles = await upload(files);
    78→
    79→        useUploadStore.getState().setRegistering();
    80→
    81→        // Build file index for mimeType lookup
    82→        const filesByName = new Map(files.map((f) => [f.name, f]));
    83→
    84→        await addFiles(
    85→          folderId,
    86→          uploadedFiles.map((uploaded) => ({
    87→            cid: uploaded.cid,
    88→            wrappedKey: uploaded.wrappedKey,
    89→            iv: uploaded.iv,
    90→            originalName: uploaded.originalName,
    91→            originalSize: uploaded.originalSize,
    92→            mimeType: filesByName.get(uploaded.originalName)?.type || 'application/octet-stream',
    93→            encryptionMode: uploaded.encryptionMode,
    94→          }))
    95→        );
    96→
    97→        useUploadStore.getState().setSuccess();
    98→        return true;
    99→      } catch (err) {
   100→        const message = (err as Error).message;
   101→        if (message !== 'Upload cancelled by user') {
   102→          useUploadStore.getState().setError(message);
   103→
   104→          // Clean up orphaned IPFS pins if upload succeeded but registration failed
   105→          if (uploadedFiles?.length) {
   106→            for (const f of uploadedFiles) {
   107→              void unpinFromIpfs(f.cid).catch(() => {});
   108→            }
   109→            useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();
   110→          }
   111→        }
   112→        return false;
   113→      }
   114→    },
   115→    [upload, canUpload, addFiles]
   116→  );
   117→
   118→  return { handleFileDrop, isUploading };
   119→}
   120→
```
